{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"tasks","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"daily task, monotonous task, regular task, parental task","type":"example"},{"text":"The employee refused to complete the assignment, arguing that it was not one of the tasks listed in her job description.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of work done as part of one’s duties."],"synonyms":[{"word":"assignment"},{"word":"chore"},{"word":"job"}],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I put him to task on the chores.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of work done as part of one’s duties.","A state of diligence in work on a task."],"synonyms":[{"word":"assignment"},{"word":"chore"},{"word":"job"}],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"carry out a task","type":"example"},{"text":"complete a task","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of work done as part of one’s duties.","Any piece of work done."],"synonyms":[{"word":"assignment"},{"word":"chore"},{"word":"job"}],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Each member of the team completed tasks in service of the goal.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of work done as part of one’s duties.","A single action undertaken by a given agent."],"synonyms":[{"word":"assignment"},{"word":"chore"},{"word":"job"}],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Getting him to change his behavior will be quite a task for you.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'm currently working on the task she gave me, alphabetizing all of those print files.","type":"example"},{"text":"Solving the economy is an enormous task for the ministry.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of work done as part of one’s duties.","A difficult or tedious undertaking; a substantial project."],"synonyms":[{"word":"assignment"},{"word":"chore"},{"word":"job"}],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Your task is the elimination of the termites.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of work done as part of one’s duties.","An objective or duty."],"synonyms":[{"word":"assignment"},{"word":"chore"},{"word":"job"},{"word":"goal"},{"word":"objective"},{"word":"see: Thesaurus:goal"}],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The user killed the frozen task.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of work done as part of one’s duties.","A process or execution of a program."],"synonyms":[{"word":"assignment"},{"word":"chore"},{"word":"job"},{"word":"process"}],"tags":["countable"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"call to task, take to task","type":"example"},{"text":"I will put him to task for what he's done.","type":"example"},{"text":"Can she hold us to task for that?","type":"example"},{"text":"I hope somebody has brought him to task by now.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["State of being subject to reprimands."],"synonyms":[{"word":"hot seat"}],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A tax or charge."],"synonyms":[{"word":"levy"}],"tags":["countable","obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"täsk","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/tɑːsk/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/task/","tags":["Northern-England","Scotland"]},{"enpr":"tăsk","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/tæsk/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":257072,"upstream_offset":1278786215,"upstream_record_sha256":"1ca9b9f78d6e5512bd24ba933912a73428632bdfbfbb08e1277a2bda4fe75c76","word":"task"}
